- **epe-2-plan**: PLAN phase of the EPE cycle. Gate: nothing is edited until the user writes "plan approved"; then it marks `Status: APPROVED` and the gate-plan hook allows editing. Use when the user says "/epe-2-plan", "make the plan", "plan approved", or after accepting an exploration. - **epe-3-execute**: EXECUTE phase of the EPE cycle. With an APPROVED plan.md, implements (or produces) tasks.md in vertical increments on its own branch, verifies each criterion with its test until truly green, writes evidence.md and validation.md (non-technical, for whoever approves), leaves a journal entry and opens the PR with the template. Never merges, never deploys, never pushes to protected branches. Use when the user says "/epe-3-execute", "implement the plan", "execute", "write the piece", after "plan approved". - **epe-4-review**: INDEPENDENT adversarial review of an EPE change (branch or PR). Edits NOTHING. Use when the user says "/epe-4-review", "review the PR", "adversarial review", "fresh eyes". - **epe-5-apply**: Applies ONLY the /epe-4-review findings the human approved, with a fixer subagent that cannot discard anything (if it believes a finding is a false positive it BOUNCES it back with an argument).
